Entry 1
U: I've got some leisure time, and I want to go to an event.
A: Can you suggest some dates, and places? How many tickets would you like?
U: I'd like to go to Philly, on the 4th of this month. I'll just want one ticket.
A: Any type of event that you fancy?
U: I'm interested in Lights, if there's any performance on the 10th.
A: So to confirm, I'll book 1 ticket for Lights, happening in Philadelphia on March 10th. Is this okay?
U: Actually, could you search for events in and around LAX?
A: I can change the booking so you have 1 ticket for the event in Los Angeles.
U: That's fine. So when and where is this event taking place?
A: The tickets have been bought. The event will be at The Teragram Ballroom, starting at 6 pm.
U: Can you find me a rental car to get there?
A: So can you give me some times when you'll require the car? Will you pick it up on March 10th?
U: I'll collect the car at two pm on the 9th of this month, and return it on 14th of March.
A: There are 4 options for you- how about a Standard model Ford Fusion, available for pickup on March 9th at LAX International Airport?
U: What's the cost?
A: In total, it will cost $175.
U: What else is available? I don't mind what type of car, so long as I can keep it until March 11th.
A: I have found 9 options for you- you could get a Compact Chevrolet Bolt, available for pick up at LAX International Airport on March 9th.
U: I like the sound of that.
A: Shall I reserve it?
U: Yes, go ahead.
A: So I'll reserve a Compact car to be picked up from LAX International Airport, at 2 pm on March 9th, and returned on March 11th. Is this okay?
U: Yes, that's good, what's the cost?
A: The car has been reserved, at a cost of $54 in total.
U: Thanks a lot.
A: Anything else I can help you with?
U: No, thanks a lot, that's all I need.
A: Have a great day!
